# RS / BM Version Differences in the Code Region (`0x08000000`–`0x08120000`)

Generated by `decomp/tools/vdiff.py` for Milestone **DC-4** (`tools/merge_bm.py` shared-source merge and segment splitting).

## 1. Executive Summary & Per-Segment Counts

Across the 1,179,648-byte (`0x120000`) Red Sun code region (`0x08000000`–`0x08120000`, corresponding to Blue Moon `0x08000000`–`0x08120014`), every difference between Red Sun (`bn4_rs_us.gba`) and Blue Moon (`bn4_bm_us.gba`) falls into either:
1. **Shift changes (insertions / deletions):** 5 functions with version-specific instructions (totaling +0x14 bytes in BM) and 3 cutscene/map script data tables in `code_04` with version-specific 14-byte `0x3E`/`0x3C` event-check commands (net +0xC, -0x4, and -0x8 bytes in BM, summing to 0 across `code_04`).
2. **Equal-length differing sites:**
   - **Shifted same-target code references:** 17,487 decoded Thumb `bl` calls (+ 15 unreached `bl` calls in dead-code `incrom` blocks), 3,832 decoded `.word` code pointers, 30,625 `incrom` code pointers (many at unaligned bytecode offsets in `code_04`–`code_10`), and 12 intra-function PC-relative branch/`ldr` displacements.
   - **True code/constant differences:** 36 decoded 16-bit instruction/halfword immediates, 10 decoded 32-bit `.word` constants, 19 non-trivial code-region pointers (2 version-specific battle object pointer slots in `code_00` + 17 intra-table pointers in `code_04`–`code_06` that cross local cutscene script insertions/deletions), and 538 constant bytes across 105 `incrom` table clusters.
   - **Data-region pointers (`0x08120000`–`0x087FFFFF`, including bit-31-flagged `0x88xxxxxx` compressed asset pointers):** 644 decoded literal-pool `.word` pointers and 5,158 `incrom` table pointers (5,802 total).

## 2. Shift Changes (Every RS→BM Delta Transition in `0x08000000`–`0x08120000`)

There are **11 code & segment-level delta transitions** (in 4 functions and 4 data tables) that affect function start addresses, plus **19 internal table delta transitions** inside the 3 cutscene script `incrom` blocks in `code_04`. In addition, 4 regions in `code_01`, `code_05`, and `code_10` have constant-delta RS/BM bytes where `survey.py` split an `incrom` or function boundary differently between versions.

### 2.3 Disassembler Boundary Artifacts (No Actual Byte Shift)

When `tools/merge_bm.py` diffs RS and BM assembly, four spots produce version blocks purely because `functions_rs.txt` and `functions_bm.txt` have slightly different label/function boundaries over identical or constant-delta bytes:
- **`code_01` (`0x08011888`–`0x08012470`, delta `+0x0`):** RS splits `BN4_ENEMY_STATS` into two `incrom` blocks (`incrom 0x11888, 0xEC` + `dat_08011974: incrom 0x11974, 0xAFC`) because `0x0800631C` references `dat_08011974` in RS (and `dat_080204C0` in BM), whereas BM emits a single `incrom 0x11888, 0xBE8`.
- **`code_05` (`0x08079908`–`0x0807B120`, delta `+0xC`):** BM splits `dat_08079914` (`0x1818` bytes) at `dat_080799F2` (`0xDE` + `0x173A`) because `0x08006324` references `dat_080799F2` in BM (and `dat_080C0F9C` in RS).
- **`code_05` (`0x08089F10`–`0x08089F9C`, delta `+0xC`):** BM has a false-positive 6-byte function entry `sub_08089F8C` inside a pointer/data block that RS keeps as `incrom 0x89F10, 0x8C` (the halfword at RS `0x08089F80` is `0xA947`, while the shifted pointer at BM `0x08089F8C` happens to be `0xB547` = `push {r0, r1, r2, r6, lr}`).
- **`code_10` (`0x08113C74`–`0x08113CB6`, delta `+0x14`):** `functions_rs.txt` has 3 mid-function entry labels (`sub_08113C88`, `sub_08113C94`, `sub_08113CB4`) that `functions_bm.txt` lacks, and BM `sub_08113CB4` stops 2 bytes earlier (`bx lr` is emitted as `.word 0x00004770` before `data_from = 0x08113CCC`).

## 3. Recommended Segment Split

Splitting `segments_rs.toml` and `segments_bm.toml` at the points below gives every code segment a **single, invariant `bm_delta` across all functions**, eliminating all shift-drift `.if VERSION_BM` blocks:

1. **`code_04` (critical — removes ~260 lines of shift-drift version blocks across 41 functions):**
   Currently `code_04` (`0x0804F21C`–`0x080711E4`) uses a single `bm_delta = 0xC`, which is wrong for the 7 functions in `0x0805D4AC`–`0x08060264` (`bm_delta = 0x18`) and the 34 functions in `0x080612C8`–`0x0806544C` (`bm_delta = 0x14`). Splitting `code_04` into 4 sub-segments aligns every function and data label:

   | Proposed Segment | RS Start | RS End | BM Start | BM End | `bm_delta` | Contents |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---:|---|
   | `code_04a` | `0x0804F21C` | `0x0805CF5C` | `0x0804F228` | `0x0805CF74` | `+0xC` | 209 functions (`sub_0804F21C`–`sub_0805AF8C`) + cutscene table 1 (`incrom 0x5AFC6`, net `+0xC`) |
   | `code_04b` | `0x0805CF5C` | `0x080612C8` | `0x0805CF74` | `0x080612DC` | `+0x18` | `dat_0805CF5C`–`dat_0805D48C`, 7 functions (`sub_0805D4AC`–`sub_08060248`), + cutscene table 2 (`incrom 0x60264`, net `-0x4`) |
   | `code_04c` | `0x080612C8` | `0x0806765C` | `0x080612DC` | `0x08067668` | `+0x14` | 34 functions (`sub_080612C8`–`sub_0806541A`) + cutscene table 3 (`incrom 0x6544C`, net `-0x8`) |
   | `code_04d` | `0x0806765C` | `0x080711E4` | `0x08067668` | `0x080711F0` | `+0xC` | `dat_0806765C` + 66 functions (`sub_08067B3C`–`sub_08070B84`) |

   *(Note: If segment starts must sit on function starts rather than `dat_` labels, `code_04b` can start at `0x0805D4AC` / BM `0x0805D4C4` and `code_04d` can start at `0x08067B3C` / BM `0x08067B48`, keeping the preceding `dat_` tables at the tail of `code_04a` and `code_04c`.)*

2. **`code_09` / `code_10` boundary (removes shift drift across the last 17 functions of `code_09`):**
   Currently `code_09` ends at RS `0x0810A1B4` (BM `0x0810A1C8`), but the shift from `+0xC` to `+0x14` happens inside `sub_08109E48` (`0x08109E48`–`0x08109ED8`), so the 17 functions in RS `0x08109EF0`–`0x0810A1B4` already have `bm_delta = 0x14`.
   - Move the `code_09` / `code_10` boundary back from RS `0x0810A1B4` (BM `0x0810A1C8`) to **RS `0x08109EF0` (BM `0x08109F04`)**, the first function after `sub_08109E48`'s literal pool.

3. **`code_02` / `code_03` and `code_03` / `code_04` boundaries (already optimal at function level):**
   - In `code_02`, the `+0x4` → `+0x8` insertion is inside the last function `sub_08035020` (`0x08035020`–`0x08035088`), right before `code_03` starts at `0x08035088` (BM `0x08035090`).
   - In `code_03`, the `+0x8` → `+0xC` insertion is inside the last function `ScriptCmd_checkGameVersion` (`0x0804F204`–`0x0804F21C`), right before `code_04` starts at `0x0804F21C` (BM `0x0804F228`).
   - In `code_01`, `BN4_FACE_GET` (`0x08016A20`–`0x08016A6C`) inserts +2 bytes at `0x08016A68` and removes 2 bytes of padding at `0x08016A6A`, so its net delta change is `0`.

## 4. Differing Sites by Kind

### 4.1 Whole Functions or Code Blocks That Differ (Length-Changing Functions)

Only **4 functions** in the entire code region have different instructions/sizes between Red Sun and Blue Moon (plus 1 false-positive function entry `sub_08089F8C` in BM `code_05` and 3 extra mid-function labels in RS `code_10` `0x08113C88`–`0x08113CB4`):

1. **`BN4_FACE_GET` (`sub_08016A20`, `code_01`, RS `0x08016A20`–`0x08016A6C` [74B + 2B pad] vs BM `0x08016A20`–`0x08016A6C` [76B]):**
   - At RS `0x08016A64`, RS computes `adds r0, r4, #0; adds r0, #4` and pops (`pop {r4, pc}; .hword 0x0000`).
   - BM adds `subs r0, #6` (`06 38`) at `0x08016A68` before `pop {r4, pc}`, consuming the 2-byte alignment pad so the literal pool at `0x08016A6C` does not shift. The 3 internal branches at `0x08016A38`, `0x08016A50`, and `0x08016A62` increase their target displacement by +2 bytes (`_08016A68` → `_08016A6A`).
2. **`sub_08035020` (BM `sub_08035024`, `code_02`, RS `0x08035020`–`0x08035088` [104B] vs BM `0x08035024`–`0x08035090` [108B]):**
   - Checks a range of IDs (`r1 = r0 & 0xFF`): RS checks `cmp r1, #0x85; beq _08035044; cmp r1, #0x7f; bge _08035084`.
   - BM checks `cmp r1, #0x79; blt _0803504C; cmp r1, #0x85; beq _0803504C; cmp r1, #0x7e; ble _0803508C` (+4 bytes). The PC-relative load at `0x0803502C` (`.hword 0x4E6A` → `0x4E6B`) adjusts its displacement by +4 bytes.
3. **`ScriptCmd_checkGameVersion` (`sub_0804F204`, BM `sub_0804F20C`, `code_03`, RS `0x0804F204`–`0x0804F21C` [24B] vs BM `0x0804F20C`–`0x0804F228` [26B + 2B pad]):**
   - RS loads script argument byte `movs r0, #2; ldrb r1, [r4, r0]`.
   - BM leaves `movs r0, #2` in place and immediately overwrites `r0` with `movs r0, #3` (+2 bytes), followed by 2 bytes of `.hword 0x0000` padding at `0x0804F226` (+4 bytes total).
4. **`sub_08109E48` (BM `sub_08109E54`, `code_09`, RS `0x08109E48`–`0x08109ED8` [144B] vs BM `0x08109E54`–`0x08109EEC` [150B + 2B pad]):**
   - At RS `0x08109EB2` (BM `0x08109EBE`), BM inserts 3 instructions (`tst r1, r1; bne _08109EC4; adds r1, #1`, 6 bytes) plus 2 bytes of `.hword 0x0000` padding before literal pool `_08109EEC` (+8 bytes total). Eight internal branch and `ldr` instructions in `sub_08109E48` adjust their PC-relative displacements accordingly.

### 4.4 Code-Region Pointers (`0x08000000`–`0x0811FFFF`)

#### A. Same-Target Shifted Code References (51,959 Total)
- **17,487 decoded Thumb `bl` calls** and **15 unreached Thumb `bl` calls inside dead-code `incrom` blocks** (`code_06` `0x080A2C86`, `code_07` `0x080B5252`/`0x080B7652`/`0x080CF4C2`, and `code_08` `0x080D5352`/`0x080D5362`/`0x080D5374`/`0x080D7606`/`0x080DD85A`/`0x080E2978`/`0x080EFF30` plus 4 others) whose 22-bit PC-relative offset changes by `-4`, `-6`, `-8`, `-12`, or `-20` bytes solely because the caller shifted while the callee is in `code_00`/`code_01` (`delta = 0`), or vice versa.
- **3,832 decoded `.word` code pointers** (literal pools and jump/function tables).
- **30,625 `incrom` code pointers** (including unaligned 32-bit script/cutscene pointers in `code_04`, `code_05`, `code_06`, `code_09`, and `code_10`) where `BM_ptr == RS_ptr + true_delta(RS_ptr)`.
